\displaystyle\|\mskip 1.5mu\mathcal{N}_{1}\mskip 1.5mu\|_{\mathscr{L}(L^{2}_{k_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\parallel$}}}(\Sigma_{\textnormal{aug}}))}=\bigg\|\mskip 1.5mu\frac{1}{\mathrm{i}\mskip 1.5mu2\pi}\int_{\{z^{\prime}\in\mathbb{C}\,/\,|\mathop{\mathfrak{Im}}\nolimits z^{\prime}|\leq\eta\}}\frac{\partial\widetilde{w}}{\partial\overline{z}}(z)\,\Big(\mskip 1.5mu\frac{\mathcal{H}^{\delta}_{{\textnormal{aug}},k_{\mathchoice{\mathrel{\mkern 1.0mu\scalebox{0.875}{$\displaystyle\parallel$}}}{\mathrel{\mkern 1.0mu\scalebox{0.875}{$\textstyle\parallel$}}}{\mathrel{\mkern 1.0mu\scalebox{0.875}{$\scriptstyle\parallel$}}}{\mathrel{\mkern 1.0mu\scalebox{0.875}{$\scriptscriptstyle\parallel$}}}}}-E_{D}}{\delta}-z\mskip 1.5mu\Big)^{-1}\,dz\wedge d\overline{z}\mskip 1.5mu\bigg\|_{\mathscr{L}(L^{2}_{k_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\parallel$}}}(\Sigma_{\textnormal{aug}}))}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\{z^{\prime}\in\mathbb{C}\,/\,|\mathop{\mathfrak{Im}}\nolimits z^{\prime}|\leq\eta\}\,\cap\,\operatorname{supp}\widetilde{w}}\bigg|\frac{\partial\widetilde{w}}{\partial\overline{z}}(z)\bigg|\,\bigg\|\Big(\mskip 1.5mu\frac{\mathcal{H}^{\delta}_{{\textnormal{aug}},k_{\mathchoice{\mathrel{\mkern 1.0mu\scalebox{0.875}{$\displaystyle\parallel$}}}{\mathrel{\mkern 1.0mu\scalebox{0.875}{$\textstyle\parallel$}}}{\mathrel{\mkern 1.0mu\scalebox{0.875}{$\scriptstyle\parallel$}}}{\mathrel{\mkern 1.0mu\scalebox{0.875}{$\scriptscriptstyle\parallel$}}}}}-E_{D}}{\delta}-z\mskip 1.5mu\Big)^{-1}\bigg\|_{\mathscr{L}(L^{2}_{k_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\parallel$}}}(\Sigma_{\textnormal{aug}}))}\,dz\wedge d\overline{z}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{C\,C_{N}}{2\pi}\,\|w^{(N+1)}\|_{\infty}\,\int_{-\eta}^{\eta}|y|^{N}\frac{1}{|y|}\,dy\lesssim\|w^{(N+1)}\|_{\infty}\,\eta^{N},

(B.4)

uniformly in
\delta
, where the last inequalities follow from the the bound (
B.2
) on
\partial_{\overline{z}}\,\widetilde{w}
and the resolvent estimate
\|(\delta^{-1}\,(\mathcal{H}^{\delta}_{{\textnormal{aug}},k_{\mathchoice{\mathrel{\mkern 1.0mu\scalebox{0.875}{$\displaystyle\parallel$}}}{\mathrel{\mkern 1.0mu\scalebox{0.875}{$\textstyle\parallel$}}}{\mathrel{\mkern 1.0mu\scalebox{0.875}{$\scriptstyle\parallel$}}}{\mathrel{\mkern 1.0mu\scalebox{0.875}{$\scriptscriptstyle\parallel$}}}}}-E_{D})-z)^{-1}\|\leq|\mathop{\mathfrak{Im}}\nolimits z|^{-1}=|y|^{-1}
. Similarly, since
\mathcal{J}_{\delta}
is bounded uniformly in
\delta
(Proposition
9.4
) we find that

\|\mskip 1.5mu\mathcal{N}_{3}\mskip 1.5mu\|_{\mathscr{L}(L^{2}_{k_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\parallel$}}}(\Sigma_{\textnormal{aug}}))}\lesssim\|w^{(N+1)}\|_{\infty}\,\eta^{N}.

(B.5)

We now bound
\mathcal{N}_{2}
. By the triangle inequality,

\|\mskip 1.5mu\mathcal{N}_{2}\mskip 1.5mu\|_{\mathscr{L}(L^{2}_{k_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\parallel$}}}(\Sigma_{\textnormal{aug}}))}\leq\frac{1}{2\pi}\,\sup_{|\mathop{\mathfrak{Im}}\nolimits z|>\eta}\|\mathscr{P}^{\delta}(z)\|_{\mathscr{L}(L^{2}_{k_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\parallel$}}}(\Sigma_{\textnormal{aug}}))}\int_{\{z^{\prime}\in\mathbb{C}\,/\,|\mathop{\mathfrak{Im}}\nolimits z^{\prime}|>\eta\}}\bigg|\frac{\partial\widetilde{w}}{\partial\overline{z}}(z)\bigg|\,dz\wedge d\overline{z},

where

\mathscr{P}^{\delta}(z):=\bigg(\mskip 1.5mu\frac{\mathcal{H}^{\delta}_{{\textnormal{aug}},k_{\mathchoice{\mathrel{\mkern 1.0mu\scalebox{0.875}{$\displaystyle\parallel$}}}{\mathrel{\mkern 1.0mu\scalebox{0.875}{$\textstyle\parallel$}}}{\mathrel{\mkern 1.0mu\scalebox{0.875}{$\scriptstyle\parallel$}}}{\mathrel{\mkern 1.0mu\scalebox{0.875}{$\scriptscriptstyle\parallel$}}}}}-E_{D}}{\delta}-z\mskip 1.5mu\bigg)^{-1}\!\!-\mathrm{e}^{\mskip 1.5mu\mathrm{i}\mskip 1.5mu\delta\mu{\mathfrak{K}}_{1}\cdot{\bm{x}}}\,\mathcal{J}^{*}_{\delta}\;\mathds{1}_{\mathbb{L}(\delta^{3/4})}\;\big(\mskip 1.5mu\mathcal{D}^{\mskip 1.5mu\delta}(\mu)-z\mskip 1.5mu\big)^{-1}\;\mathds{1}_{\mathbb{L}(\delta^{3/4})}\;\mathcal{J}_{\delta}\,\mathrm{e}^{-\mathrm{i}\mskip 1.5mu\delta\mu{\mathfrak{K}}_{1}\cdot{\bm{x}}}.

If
z
satisfies
\mathop{\mathfrak{Im}}\nolimits z\geq\eta
, then its distance to the spectrum of
\mathcal{D}^{\mskip 1.5mu\delta}(\mu)
is bounded from below by
1/\eta
. Thus, by Theorem
7.1
, there exists
\delta_{\star}(\eta,\mu_{0})>0
such that for any
\delta\in(0,\delta_{\star}(\eta,\mu_{0}))
,

\sup_{|\mathop{\mathfrak{Im}}\nolimits z|>\eta}\|\mathscr{P}^{\delta}(z)\|_{\mathscr{L}(L^{2}_{k_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\parallel$}}}(\Sigma_{\textnormal{aug}}))}\leq C\,\eta^{-1}\,\delta^{1/4}.

It follows that

\displaystyle\|\mskip 1.5mu\mathcal{N}_{2}\mskip 1.5mu\|_{\mathscr{L}(L^{2}_{k_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\parallel$}}}(\Sigma_{\textnormal{aug}}))}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{2\pi}\,\eta^{-1}\,\delta^{1/4}\,\int_{\{z^{\prime}\in\mathbb{C}\,/\,|\mathop{\mathfrak{Im}}\nolimits z^{\prime}|>\eta\}\,\cap\,\operatorname{supp}\widetilde{w}}\bigg|\frac{\partial\widetilde{w}}{\partial\overline{z}}(z)\bigg|\,dz\wedge d\overline{z}

\displaystyle\hskip-42.67912pt\leq\frac{CC_{N}}{2\pi}\,\|w^{(N+1)}\|_{\infty}\,\eta^{-1}\,\delta^{1/4}\,\int_{\{z^{\prime}\in\mathbb{C}\,/\,|\mathop{\mathfrak{Im}}\nolimits z^{\prime}|>\eta\}\,\cap\,\operatorname{supp}\widetilde{w}}|\mathop{\mathfrak{Im}}\nolimits z|^{N}\,dz\wedge d\overline{z}\quad\textnormal{by \eqref{eq:almost_analytic_extension_bound}}

\displaystyle\hskip-42.67912pt\lesssim\|w^{(N+1)}\|_{\infty}\,\eta^{-1}\,\delta^{1/4}.

(B.6)

Combining the estimates (
B.4
)–(
B.6
), we find that for
\delta\in(0,\delta_{\star}(\eta,\mu_{0}))
,

\bigg\|\mskip 1.5muw\mskip 1.5mu\Big(\mskip 1.5mu\frac{\mathcal{H}^{\delta}_{{\textnormal{aug}},k_{\mathchoice{\mathrel{\mkern 1.0mu\scalebox{0.875}{$\displaystyle\parallel$}}}{\mathrel{\mkern 1.0mu\scalebox{0.875}{$\textstyle\parallel$}}}{\mathrel{\mkern 1.0mu\scalebox{0.875}{$\scriptstyle\parallel$}}}{\mathrel{\mkern 1.0mu\scalebox{0.875}{$\scriptscriptstyle\parallel$}}}}}-E_{D}}{\delta}\mskip 1.5mu\Big)-\mathrm{e}^{\mskip 1.5mu\mathrm{i}\mskip 1.5mu\delta\mu{\mathfrak{K}}_{1}\cdot{\bm{x}}}\,\mathcal{J}^{*}_{\delta}\;\mathds{1}_{\mathbb{L}(\delta^{3/4})}\;w(\mathcal{D}^{\mskip 1.5mu\delta}(\mu))\;\mathds{1}_{\mathbb{L}(\delta^{3/4})}\;\mathcal{J}_{\delta}\,\mathrm{e}^{-\mathrm{i}\mskip 1.5mu\delta\mu{\mathfrak{K}}_{1}\cdot{\bm{x}}}\mskip 1.5mu\bigg\|_{\mathscr{L}(L^{2}_{k_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\parallel$}}}(\Sigma_{\textnormal{aug}}))}\\
\lesssim\|w^{(N+1)}\|_{\infty}\,(\eta^{N}+\eta^{-1}\,\delta^{1/4}).

(B.7)

Finally, setting
\delta_{0}:=\min(\eta^{4(N+1)},\delta_{\star}(\eta,\mu_{0}))
so that
\eta^{-1}\,\delta^{1/4}\leq\eta^{N}
for any
\delta\in(0,\delta_{0})
, it follows that the right-hand side of (
B.7
) is bounded by
\|w^{(N+1)}\|_{\infty}\,\eta^{N}
whenever
\delta\in(0,\delta_{0})
.

To finish, we rewrite the second term on the left-hand side of (
B.7
). Because
w
is compactly supported in
(-\theta_{\textnormal{gap}},\theta_{\textnormal{gap}})
and the spectrum of
\mathcal{D}^{\delta}(\mu)
in that interval is pure point,

w\mskip 1.5mu(\mathcal{D}^{\mskip 1.5mu\delta}(\mu))=\sum_{j=-N}^{N}\begin{pmatrix}\ddots&&(0)\\
&w(z_{j}(\mu+\delta^{-1}\gamma_{\mathbf{I}}))\,\alpha^{{\mathbf{K}}_{\mathbf{I}}}(\cdot;\mu+\delta^{-1}\gamma_{\mathbf{I}})\otimes\alpha^{{\mathbf{K}}_{\mathbf{I}}}(\cdot;\mu+\delta^{-1}\gamma_{\mathbf{I}})&\\
(0)&&\ddots\end{pmatrix}.

Combining this expression with the definition (
7.7
) of
\Psi^{\delta,(0)}_{{\textnormal{aug}},{\mathbf{I}},j}
shows that the second term on the left-hand side of (
B.7
) coincides with the second term on the left-hand side of (
B.3
). This completes the proof.
